Core evaluations:
```
def compute_all_lags(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx
def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_all_lags(h,n)))
```
But for many evaluations, computing all lags O(n^2). Candidate enumeration many n. Fine. We can optimize for anti-sym maybe analytic? Not needed.

Feasibility exact/realistic:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
       diff = target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff >0:
          mask = h < 1-1e-15
          if not mask.any(): break
          # distribute proportionally to room?
          room = 1-h
          amt = diff / room[mask].sum()
          h[mask] = np.minimum(1.0, h[mask] + amt*room[mask])
       else:
          mask = h > 1e-15
          if not mask.any(): break
          h[mask] = np.maximum(0.0, h[mask] + diff * h[mask] / h[mask].sum())
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # exact adjustment
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       # choose index with most slack in direction
       if diff>0:
          order = np.argsort(1-h)
       else: order=np.argsort(h)
       for idx in order:
          # add as much as possible to sum diff exactly within bounds
          max_add = 1-h[idx] if diff>0 else h[idx]
          add = min(max_add, diff) if diff>0 else max(-max_add, diff)
          h[idx]+=add; diff-=add; if abs(diff)<1e-15 break
    return h
```
But if we distribute proportional to room, repeated may overshoot; easier use bisection projection to box+sum. For [0,1] and sum, Euclidean projection can be done by threshold: find θ such that sum(clip(h-θ,0,1))=target. Use binary search. Good, O(n log). Implement:
```

```

def make_antisym_tent(n):
    n=int(n); m=n//2

def active_smooth_obj(x, m, idx_lags, alpha):
   h = make_full(x,m)
   c = compute_all_lags(h, n)  # or only idx?
   vals = c[idx_lags]
   # stable logsumexp alpha
   return vals.max + logsumexp(alpha*(vals-vals.max))/alpha

def active_obj_grad(x,m,idx,alpha): # need gradient; if using numerical? L-BFGS-B can finite difference too slow. Provide analytic gradient.
```

Active-set loop:
```
def optimize_anti_active(a,m,deadline):
   n=2*m
   h=make_anti(a,m)
   best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_a=a.copy()
   for round...
     c=compute_all_lags(h,n)
     # choose top K lags, maybe all near max
     threshold=best_c - 0.02
     idx=np.where(c > threshold)[0]; if len<2 add top
     K=min(30,len)
     idx=idx[np.argsort(-c[idx])[:K]]
     for alpha in [20,100,500,2000]:
       res=minimize(_active_obj, a, args=(m,idx,alpha), jac=_active_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=...)
       new_a=clip; c_new=exact; if improve update, break
     if c stable break
   return best_a,best_c
```

def _stable_soft(vals, alpha):
    mv=np.max(vals)
    sh=alpha*(vals-mv)
    # if sh.max()>500? not if vals diff small; but if alpha huge and diff 1, sh 1000; handle with shift
    mx=sh.max()
    return float(mv + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))/alpha)
```

Let's design `optimize_anti_active` with line search and acceptance:
- `a_curr` initialized.
- For iteration up to time:
  - `c = all_lags(make_full(a_curr))`; `rmax = np.argmax(c)`.
  - choose idx = top K with c >= rmax - tol (tol maybe 1e-3 + 1e-4*rmax). If number < max(2,K/2), include top down.

For a list of lags (signed), for each lag compute sum with vectorized overlap:
```
def _lag_value(h,n,lag):
   dx=2/n; m=abs(lag)
   if m==0: return dx*np.dot(h,1-h)
   if lag>0:
       return dx*np.dot(h[m:], 1-h[:n-m])
   else:
       return dx*np.dot(h[:n-m], 1-h[m:])  # for lag negative: h_{i+m?}
```
Check lag<0: c=Σ h_i(1-h_{i+|lag|}) for i=0..n-m-1? From formula lag=-m c=Σ_{i=0}^{n-1} h_{i-m}(1-h_i), valid i=m..n-1 => Σ_{j=0}^{n-m-1} h_j(1-h_{j+m}). That's `np.dot(h[:n-m],1-h[m:])`. Yes.
This O(K n) for active vals. For exact we need full max, use np.correlate O(n^2). During optimization, we can compute active vals only. def _anti_full(a, m):
   h=np.empty(int(2*m)); h[:m]=a; h[m:]=1.0-a; return h

def _active_vals(a,m,idx_lags): # idx positions
   h=_anti_full(a,m); n=2*m; dx=2.0/n
   vals=np.empty(len(idx_lags))
   for t, r in enumerate(idx_lags):
      lag=r-(n-1)
      if lag==0: vals[t]=dx*float(np.dot(h,1-h))
      elif lag>0: vals[t]=dx*float(np.dot(h[lag:],1-h[:n-lag]))
      else: vals[t]=dx*float(np.dot(h[:n+lag],1-h[-lag:])) # since lag=-m, n+lag=n-m
   return vals
```

Soft max function stable:
```
def _soft_max(vals, alpha):
   v=vals.max()
   sh=alpha*(vals-v)
   mx=sh.max()
   s=np.exp(sh-mx).sum()
   return float(v + (mx + np.log(s))/alpha)
def _soft_weights(vals, alpha): ...
```

def _lp_step(h, n, trust):
    c = compute_all_lags(h,n)
    dx=2/n
    rows=[]; b=[]

       lag = r-(n-1)
       g = _lag_grad_no_dx(h,n,lag)
       rhs = c[r] - dx*(g @ h)
       row = np.empty(n+1); row[:n]=dx*g; row[n]=-1.0
       rows.append(row); b.append(rhs)
    if trust is not None:
       for i in range(n): row zeros...

def make_full_quarter(b, q):
    m=2*q; n=4*q
    a=np.empty(m)
    a[:q]=b
    a[q:]=1.0-b[::-1]
    h=np.empty(n)
    h[:m]=a
    h[m:]=1.0-a
    return h
```

Stable soft max for active vals:
```
def _soft_max(vals, alpha):
    v = float(np.max(vals))
    if alpha == 0: return v
    shifted = alpha*(vals-v)
    mx = float(np.max(shifted))
    shifted -= mx
    return v + (mx + math.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(shifted))))) / alpha
```
Use math.log.
Weights:
```
def _soft_weights(vals, alpha):
    v=np.max(vals); sh=alpha*(vals-v); mx=sh.max(); sh-=mx; w=np.exp(sh); w/=w.sum(); return w
```
If len=1, weight 1.

def adjust_sum(h,n):
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(1000):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(h<1-1e-12)[0]
          if len==0: break
          # add diff cyclically? simple pick argmax(1-h)
          add=max? 
       else...
    # distribute to multiple to keep bounds exact
```
Simpler: Euclidean projection with one bisection. But for our near exact sum, no need. However if checker requires exact sum within 1e-6, floating sum fine. We can skip projection for candidates to preserve symmetry; final adjust tiny:
```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
   target=n/2.0
   diff = target - h.sum()
   if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
      # spread over all not at bounds
      if diff>0: mask=h<1.0-1e-12
      else: mask=h>1e-12
      if mask.any():
          room = (1-h[mask]).sum() if diff>0 else h[mask].sum()
          frac = diff/room
          h[mask] = np.clip(h[mask] + frac*(1-h[mask] if diff>0 else -h[mask]?),0,1)
      # exact loop
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)>1e-13:
         order=np.argsort(1-h) if diff>0 else np.argsort(h)
         for idx in order:
            if diff>0:
               add=min(1-h[idx], diff)
            else: add=max(h[idx], diff)
            h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
            if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
   return h
```
If room sum zero not happen. This may break exact symmetry tiny. Good.

Do we need `project_feasible` for parent? Parent might not sum exactly. Use `project_feasible_fast` (bisection) before evaluating. For candidate, use enforce.

Feasibility bounds: clip.

Let's ensure no lambdas in `enforce_sum`, `project_feasible` may use nested `s` function? That is closure! The rule says no closures or lambdas. We must avoid nested functions. For bisection projection, implement without inner function? We can compute sum in loop. No nested.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1).astype(float)
    target=0.5*n
    lo=-1.0; hi=2.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0,1))
       if s>target:
          hi=mid? Need direction. x=clip(h-θ). If θ increases, x decreases, sum decreases. If s > target, need increase θ, so lo=mid?     x=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # exact adjust
    return enforce_sum(x,n)

`_lag_grad_no_dx` top-level:
```
def _lag_grad_no_dx(h,n,lag):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if lag==0:
       g=1.0-2.0*h
    else:
       m=abs(lag)
       if lag>0:
          g[:n-m] -= h[m:]
          g[m:] += (1.0-h[:n-m])
       else:
          g[:n-m] += (1.0-h[m:])
          g[m:] -= h[:n-m]
    return g
```
This allocates per lag. In jac for up to120 lags, q512 => 120*4096 ~500k zeros, okay but maybe heavy. Could accumulate with shifts without allocating per lag? We can loop and add. For performance, allocate g once? But per lag different. We can write expression directly add to grad_h:
For lag>0:
```
grad_h[:n-m] -= wt*h[m:]
grad_h[m:] += wt*(1-h[:n-m])
```
lag<0:
```
grad_h[:n-m] += wt*(1-h[m:])
grad_h[m:] -= wt*h[:n-m]
```
lag0:
```
grad_h += wt*(1-2h)
```
No per-lag allocation. Implement in jac loop. This is faster and no extra.

Implementation details:
```
def compute_all_lags(h, n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx
```
For very large n, np.correlate direct could be O(n^2). n=2048 okay. If candidate list includes n=4096? We choose 2048 max.

Mapping with slices for q=1: `grad_h[m-1:0:-1]`: m=2 -> indices 1 down to1? slice `1:0:-1` length1 [1]. Good. `grad_h[n-1:n-1-q:-1]`: n=4, q=1 -> `3:2:-1` length1 [3]. Good. For q=0 not.

`make_full_quarter`: if q=0? not.

`quarter_from_h`: For N maybe not multiple 4. Use if N%4==0. Else interpolate to q maybe 128? Let's implement a generic:
```
def quarter_from_h(h):
    N=len(h)
    q = max(8, min(128, N//4 if N%4==0 else 64))
    n_target=4*q
    x_old=(np.arange(N)+0.5)*2.0/N
    x_new=(np.arange(n_target)+0.5)*2.0/n_target
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    m=2*q
    a=0.5*(h_new[:m] + 1.0 - h_new[m:])
    b=0.5*(a[:q] + 1.0 - a[q:][::-1])
    return np.clip(b,0,1), q
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def get_parent():
    try:
        parent = initial_h_values
    except Exception:
        return None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
        if parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size < 4:
            return None
        return parent.copy()
    except Exception:
        return None


def enforce_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(50):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-14)
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            room = float((1.0 - h[mask]).sum())
            if room <= 0.0:
                break
            frac = diff / room
            h[mask] = np.clip(h[mask] + frac * (1.0 - h[mask]), 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-14
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            mass = float(h[mask].sum())
            if mass <= 0.0:
                break
            frac = diff / mass
            h[mask] = np.clip(h[mask] + frac * h[mask], 0.0, 1.0)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                break
            if diff > 0.0:
                add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
            else:
                add = max(h[idx], diff)
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add

    return h


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        proj = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(proj.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    x = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    x = enforce_sum(x, n)
    return x


def compute_all_lags(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def compute_c5(h, n):
    c = compute_all_lags(h, n)
    return float(np.max(c))


def make_full_quarter(b, q):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).copy()
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1.0)
    q = int(q)
    if q < 1:
        q = 1
        b = np.array([0.5], dtype=float)

    m = 2 * q
    n = 4 * q

    a = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    a[:q] = b
    a[q:] = 1.0 - b[::-1]

    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = a
    h[m:] = 1.0 - a
    return h


def quarter_from_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_old = int(h.size)

    if n_old >= 4 and n_old % 4 == 0:
        q = max(8, n_old // 4)
    else:
        q = max(8, (n_old + 3) // 4)
    q = min(q, 1024)
    n_new = 4 * q

    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)

    m = 2 * q
    a = 0.5 * (h_new[:m] + 1.0 - h_new[m:])
    b = 0.5 * (a[:q] + 1.0 - a[q:][::-1])
    return np.clip(b, 0.0, 1.0), q


def _cosine_quarter(q):
    q = int(q)
    x = (np.arange(q, dtype=float) + 0.5) / (2.0 * float(q))
    return np.clip(0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x), 0.0, 1.0)


def _other_quarter_starts(q, rng):
    q = int(q)
    starts = []
    u = (np.arange(q, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(q)

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        starts.append(np.clip(0.5 * (u ** p), 0.0, 1.0))

    starts.append(np.clip(0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * u), 0.0, 1.0))
    starts.append(np.clip(0.5 - 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * u), 0.0, 1.0))

    base = _cosine_quarter(q)
    for scale in (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05):
        starts.append(np.clip(base + scale * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, q), 0.0, 1.0))

    return starts


def eval_quarter(b, q):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    q = int(q)
    h = make_full_quarter(b, q)
    return compute_c5(h, 4 * q)


def finalize_quarter(b, q):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    q = int(q)
    h = make_full_quarter(b, q)
    n = 4 * q
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * n) > 1e-10:
        h = enforce_sum(h, n)
    return h, compute_c5(h, n)


def _cand_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _soft_max(vals, alpha):
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    v = float(np.max(vals))
    shifted = float(alpha) * (vals - v)
    mx = float(np.max(shifted))
    shifted = shifted - mx
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return v
    return float(v + (mx + math.log(s)) / float(alpha))


def _soft_weights(vals, alpha):
    if vals.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    v = float(np.max(vals))
    shifted = float(alpha) * (vals - v)
    mx = float(np.max(shifted))
    shifted = shifted - mx
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        w2 = np.zeros(vals.size, dtype=float)
        w2[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        return w2
    w /= s
    return w


def _active_vals_from_h(h, n, idx):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)
    vals = np.empty(idx.size, dtype=float)

    for t in range(idx.size):
        lag = int(idx[t]) - (n - 1)
        if lag == 0:
            vals[t] = dx * float(np.dot(h, 1.0 - h))
        elif lag > 0:
            nm = n - lag
            vals[t] = dx * float(np.dot(h[lag:], 1.0 - h[:nm]))
        else:
            mm = -lag
            nm = n - mm
            vals[t] = dx * float(np.dot(h[:nm], 1.0 - h[mm:]))

    return vals


def _quarter_obj(b, q, idx, alpha):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    q = int(q)
    h = make_full_quarter(b, q)
    n = 4 * q
    vals = _active_vals_from_h(h, n, idx)
    return _soft_max(vals, alpha)


def _quarter_jac(b, q, idx, alpha):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    q = int(q)
    h = make_full_quarter(b, q)
    n = 4 * q
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    vals = _active_vals_from_h(h, n, idx)
    w = _soft_weights(vals, alpha)

    grad_h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for t in range(idx.size):
        wt = float(w[t])
        if wt <= 1e-13:
            continue
        lag = int(idx[t]) - (n - 1)
        if lag == 0:
            grad_h += wt * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif lag > 0:
            nm = n - lag
            grad_h[:nm] -= wt * h[lag:]
            grad_h[lag:] += wt * (1.0 - h[:nm])
        else:
            mm = -lag
            nm = n - mm
            grad_h[:nm] += wt * (1.0 - h[mm:])
            grad_h[mm:] -= wt * h[:nm]

    grad_h *= dx

    m = 2 * q
    # b_j affects h[j], h[m+j] negatively, h[m-1-j] negatively, h[n-1-j] positively.
    grad_b = (
        grad_h[:q]
        - grad_h[m - 1:0:-1]
        - grad_h[m:m + q]
        + grad_h[n - 1:n - 1 - q:-1]
    )
    return grad_b


def optimize_quarter(b, q, deadline, k_active=160, max_iters=90):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).copy()
    q = int(q)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = 4 * q

    best_b = b.copy()
    best_h = make_full_quarter(best_b, q)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    alphas = (20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1200.0, 4800.0)
    k_active = int(min(max(24, k_active), max(32, n // 2)))

    any_improve = False
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break

        h = make_full_quarter(best_b, q)
        c = compute_all_lags(h, n)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))

        near = np.where(c >= (cmax - max(1e-6, 0.004)))[0]
        if near.size > 0:
            if near.size < k_active:
                top = np.argsort(c)[-k_active:]
                idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((near, top)))
            else:
                idx = near
        else:
            idx = np.argsort(c)[-k_active:]

        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

        idx = idx[np.argsort(-c[idx])]
        if idx.size > k_active:
            idx = idx[:k_active]

        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * q
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _quarter_obj,
                best_b.copy(),
                args=(q, idx, alpha),
                jac=_quarter_jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": max_iters, "ftol": 1e-14, "maxls": 40},
            )
            b_try = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
            time_left = deadline - time.time()
            if time_left <= 1.0:
                break
            h_try = make_full_quarter(b_try, q)
            c_try = compute_c5(h_try, n)
            if c_try < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = float(c_try)
                best_b = b_try.copy()
                best_h = h_try.copy()
                any_improve = True
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If possible, try a few very small deterministic perturbations to escape flat minima.
    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        scales = (1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3)
        for scale in scales:
            if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            rng = np.random.RandomState(12345 + scale * 1e6)
            for _ in range(3):
                if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
                    break
                b_try = np.clip(best_b + scale * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, q), 0.0, 1.0)
                h_try = make_full_quarter(b_try, q)
                c_try = compute_c5(h_try, n)
                if c_try < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = float(c_try)
                    best_b = b_try.copy()
                    best_h = h_try.copy()

    return best_b, best_h, float(best_c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")

    parent = get_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        n_parent = int(parent.size)
        try:
            p = project_feasible(parent, n_parent)
            c = compute_c5(p, n_parent)
            best_h = p.copy()
            best_c = float(c)

            bq, qq = quarter_from_h(p)
            c_q = eval_quarter(bq, qq)
            # Keep parent information for later if it is competitive.
            parent_quarter = (float(c_q), bq, int(qq))
        except Exception:
            parent_quarter = None
    else:
        parent_quarter = None

    candidates = []
    if parent_quarter is not None:
        candidates.append(parent_quarter)

    q_list = [16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96, 128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768, 1024]

    for q in q_list:
        if time.time() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        try:
            b0 = _cosine_quarter(q)
            c0 = eval_quarter(b0, q)
            candidates.append((float(c0), b0, int(q)))

            if q <= 128 and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
                starts = _other_quarter_starts(q, rng)
                for b in starts:
                    if time.time() > deadline - 25.0:
                        break
                    cc = eval_quarter(b, q)
                    if cc < 0.42:
                        candidates.append((float(cc), np.asarray(b, dtype=float).copy(), int(q)))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if len(candidates) == 0:
        q = 128
        b0 = _cosine_quarter(q)
        h0 = make_full_quarter(b0, q)
        h0 = enforce_sum(h0, 4 * q)
        c0 = compute_c5(h0, 4 * q)
        return h0, float(c0), int(4 * q)

    candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)

    # Update best with the strongest exact candidates before slower refinement.
    for c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate in candidates:
        if c_candidate >= best_c:
            break
        try:
            h_candidate, c_final = finalize_quarter(b_candidate, q_candidate)
            if c_final < best_c:
                best_h = h_candidate
                best_c = float(c_final)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if best_c <= 0.3760:
            break

    # Slightly diversify around the best shapes.
    selected = []
    seen_q = set()
    for c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate in candidates:
        if c_candidate > best_c + 0.02:
            break
        if q_candidate in seen_q:
            continue
        seen_q.add(q_candidate)
        selected.append((c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate))
        if len(selected) >= 6:
            break

    for c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate in selected[:4]:
        if time.time() > deadline - 90.0:
            break
        for scale in (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02):
            if time.time() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            for _ in range(2):
                if time.time() > deadline - 90.0:
                    break
                b_try = np.clip(b_candidate + scale * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, q_candidate), 0.0, 1.0)
                c_try = eval_quarter(b_try, q_candidate)
                if c_try <= best_c:
                    candidates.append((float(c_try), b_try.copy(), int(q_candidate)))

    if len(candidates) > 0:
        candidates.sort(key=_cand_key)
        for c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate in candidates:
            if c_candidate >= best_c:
                break
            h_candidate, c_final = finalize_quarter(b_candidate, q_candidate)
            if c_final < best_c:
                best_h = h_candidate
                best_c = float(c_final)

    # Refine a few unique, promising constructions.
    opt_selected = []
    seen_q2 = set()
    for c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate in candidates:
        if c_candidate > best_c + 0.005:
            break
        if q_candidate in seen_q2:
            continue
        if q_candidate > 512:
            # Very large unstructured cosine is already near-optimal; do not spend
            # significant nonlinear optimization time there.
            if len(opt_selected) >= 4:
                break
            if c_candidate <= best_c + 1e-6 and q_candidate in (768, 1024):
                opt_selected.append((c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate))
                seen_q2.add(q_candidate)
            continue
        seen_q2.add(q_candidate)
        opt_selected.append((c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate))
        if len(opt_selected) >= 5:
            break

    for rank, item in enumerate(opt_selected):
        c_candidate, b_candidate, q_candidate = item
        remaining = deadline - time.time()
        if remaining < 60.0:
            break
        if c_candidate > best_c + 0.01:
            continue

        if q_candidate >= 768:
            alloc = min(80.0, remaining / 2.0)
        else:
            alloc = min(260.0, remaining / float(max(1, len(opt_selected) - rank)))
        alloc = max(25.0, alloc)

        sub_deadline = time.time() + alloc
        try:
            b_opt, h_opt, c_opt = optimize_quarter(
                b_candidate,
                q_candidate,
                sub_deadline,
                k_active=160,
                max_iters=90,
            )
            h_finish, c_finish = finalize_quarter(b_opt, q_candidate)
            if c_finish < best_c:
                best_h = h_finish
                best_c = float(c_finish)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final safety polish: if the best solution is still significantly above target,
    # try one more generalized refinement on the best quarter construction.
    if best_h is not None and best_c > 0.3808 and time.time() < deadline - 45.0:
        n_best = int(best_h.size)
        b_best, q_best = quarter_from_h(best_h)
        try:
            b_opt, h_opt, c_opt = optimize_quarter(
                b_best,
                q_best,
                deadline - 2.0,
                k_active=200,
                max_iters=120,
            )
            h_finish, c_finish = finalize_quarter(b_opt, q_best)
            if c_finish < best_c:
                best_h = h_finish
                best_c = float(c_finish)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is None:
        q = 256
        b0 = _cosine_quarter(q)
        best_h = make_full_quarter(b0, q)

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    n_best = int(best_h.size)
    best_h = enforce_sum(best_h, n_best)
    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_best)

    return best_h, float(best_c), n_best
```
